- **uberscan**: Whole-codebase read-only audit. Packs the repo into a fixed fleet of byte-balanced areas (default 8) and runs ONE multi-lens reviewer per area, plus an inline repo-global Semgrep + test-coverage pass. Aggregates findings into a markdown report and files deduped GitHub issues. Never writes code. Whole-repo by default; pass a path/glob to narrow. ([UberDevCorp/UberDev](https://github.com/UberDevCorp/UberDev/blob/HEAD/plugins/uberdev/commands/uberscan.md) · ★2)
- **ubersimplify**: Whole-codebase 3-lens simplification (Reuse/Quality/Efficiency). Packs the repo into a fixed fleet of byte-balanced areas (default 8), audits each area with ONE multi-lens code-simplifier, applies preserve-behavior fixes via code-fixer as one refactor: commit per area on a new branch, opens ONE PR for review, and files leftover blocker findings as GitHub issues. Whole-repo by default; pass a path/glob to narrow. --audit-only for a read-only scan. ([UberDevCorp/UberDev](https://github.com/UberDevCorp/UberDev/blob/HEAD/plugins/uberdev/commands/ubersimplify.md) · ★2)
- **uberthink**: Read-only cross-domain ideation engine. Fans an agent fleet over parallel islands to generate, gap-fill, combine, and falsify novel approaches across donor domains (SWE/gamedev/IT/math + far-field wildcards). Emits a ranked dossier with a moonshot lane and files top ideas as GitHub issues. ([UberDevCorp/UberDev](https://github.com/UberDevCorp/UberDev/blob/HEAD/plugins/uberdev/commands/uberthink.md) · ★2)

- **uitool.inspect**: 1. Resolve `&lt;app&gt;` to its live session over the socket ([[domain.uitool.ipc]]); no session is `NOT_ATTACHED` (exit 4). 2. Send the `inspect` op with the node id, the `--invoke` flag, and the `--match` pattern. 3. The server resolves the node id to its live object through the [[domain.uitool.registry]] — re-walking the structural path and passing the four-gate validation (epoch, path, pointer valid ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0007-inspect/commands/uitool.inspect.md))
- **uitool.launch**: `launch` brings a target up **fresh** with the inspector already loaded — the cooperative **launch path** ([[domain.uitool.injection]]): `posix_spawn` under `DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES=…/UIToolBoot.dylib` so the [[domain.uitool.boot]] dylib loads before `main` and starts the [[domain.uitool.server]]. Use it when a clean launch state is wanted. To inspect an app **as it sits right now**, preserving its ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0002-attach/commands/uitool.launch.md))
- **uitool.list-apps**: `list-apps` is **pure local detection** — like [[command.uitool.doctor]] it runs before any injection and does **not** open the IPC socket or contact a target ([[domain.uitool.ipc]] is the post-injection protocol, unused here). It enumerates the running processes that are candidates for attachment and annotates each: ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0001-doctor/commands/uitool.list-apps.md))
- **uitool.node**: The drill step of the canonical loop (locate few → project narrow → **read deep on the survivors**). Reads a single node the agent has already located and pulls the on-demand facets the default tree/find projection omits. It does not walk a subtree and does not search. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0006-node/commands/uitool.node.md))
- **uitool.schema**: No target, no `--snapshot`, no injection — `schema` is a pure static read of the contract the other verbs emit. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0008-schema/commands/uitool.schema.md))
- **uitool.signing**: No injection, no attach — `signing` reads the signature off the target's binary. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0009-signing/commands/uitool.signing.md))
- **uitool.tree**: The structural-exploration verb. Walks the view hierarchy from a chosen root down a finite depth, projects a chosen set of fields per node, and emits an explicit truncated marker at every branch cut off by the depth limit. It never walks the whole tree — `--depth` is always finite and small by default. The coding agent is the consumer; output is machine-first. ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0004-tree/commands/uitool.tree.md))
- **uitool.windows**: 1. Resolve `&lt;app&gt;` to the attached session's socket per [[domain.uitool.ipc]]; if no session is attached, fail per [[domain.uitool.ipc]]. 2. Issue the [[domain.uitool.ipc]] `hierarchy` op with `maxDepth: 0` and `window: "all"` to the injected server. `maxDepth: 0` projects window roots only — no descendant walk — which is exactly what this verb returns; there is no dedicated `windows` op. The serv ([markmals/apple-platform-tools](https://github.com/markmals/apple-platform-tools/blob/HEAD/Features/uitool/0003-windows/commands/uitool.windows.md))
